# Pine Script® v6 Coverage Matrix

Each row describes a PineWASM feature, cites tests, and identifies known gaps. A
`verified` row has an end-to-end test that asserts the runtime values described
in its notes. The totals count features, rather than overloads or
the entire language. Check the linked tests before updating a
support claim. For example, the `once` tests check when a block runs, the
comparison tests check six operators near the equality threshold, and the SMA,
VWMA, and KCW rows identify the inputs and outputs covered by their tests. See the [Pine Script® v6 manual](https://www.tradingview.com/pine-script-docs/)
and [language reference](https://www.tradingview.com/pine-script-reference/v6/)
for the language specification.

## Status legend

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `unsupported` | Not implemented. Using it produces a diagnostic (usually `E0201`/`E0202`) or a parse error. |
| `partial` | Some of the v6 surface for this feature works; the Notes column says exactly which part. |
| `supported` | Implemented and covered by at least a compiler-level test (parser/semantics/CSIL). |
| `verified` | The behavior described in Notes is covered by an end-to-end test that asserts runtime *values*, not just successful compilation. This does not imply complete coverage beyond that stated scope. |

**Rules:**

1. Every `supported` or `verified` row **must** link at least one covering test or fixture
   in its Tests column. A row with no test cannot claim more than `partial`.
2. No support claim without a test. Where the language documentation does not fully
   specify a behavior (or no test pins it yet), we encode our chosen behavior as a test
   and mark the row **"assumed"** in Notes — the test pins *our* semantics, not the language's.
3. Rows whose exact name has not been checked against the linked tests carry
   the note *"unverified name — confirm against the linked tests"*. Better flagged than
   fabricated.
4. For `unsupported` rows the Tests column is "—".
5. Every Tests-column entry used as coverage evidence links to a test or
   fixture in this repository. Every status rests on reproducible linked evidence
   in this repository.
6. A runtime-value test does not erase a known limitation. If a row omits an
   overload or feature behavior, its status is `partial` even when the implemented
   subset is verified end to end.

The counts describe evidence coverage for the scope stated in each row; they are not a
percentage-complete or overall coverage score. The partial rows identify concrete
boundaries such as accepted-but-unapplied declaration metadata, missing overloads,
restricted collection/type surfaces, host-dependent request behavior, incomplete
formatting, and simplified timeframe/session semantics. Documented limitations also
remain in some verified rows where the implemented behavior itself is value-tested.

## 1. Language surface

### 1.1 Directives & declarations

| Feature | Status | Tests | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `//@version=6` directive | verified | [`parse_version_directive_value`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`golden_close_difference_example`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`core_example.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/core_example.pine) | May appear on any top-level line, including after code or imports; placement regression: [`version_directive_can_follow_top_level_code`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs); runtime values asserted by the golden test. |
| Script without `//@version` directive | verified | [`builtin_series_and_bar_index`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`no_version_no_indicator.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/no_version_no_indicator.pine) | Treated as v6; runtime values asserted by the VM test (directive-less source). |
| `//@version=N` with N < 6 | verified | [`version_directive_below_6_is_rejected_with_e0249`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Rejected with `E0249` and a pointer to `pinewasm migrate`. |
| `//@version=N` with N > 6 | verified | [`version_directive_above_6_warns_w0003_and_compiles_as_v6`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | **Note:** warns (`W0003`) and compiles as v6. |
| Repeated `//@version` directive | verified | [`duplicate_version_directive_warns_w0005`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs) | PineWASM keeps the first one and warns `W0005` on each repeat instead of failing the parse. |
| `indicator(title)` — const string title | verified | [`golden_close_difference_example`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`indicator_with_non_string_title_is_rejected`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`indicator_title_may_be_named`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`core_example.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/core_example.pine) | Title must be a `const string`: a literal, a `const` alias or any const-folding string expression. A wrong type is `E0205`; an `input`, `simple` or `series` string is `E0206` ([`series_value_for_a_const_parameter_is_e0206`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_invalid_parameter_diagnostics.rs), [`wrong_type_is_e0205`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_invalid_parameter_diagnostics.rs) and [`input_value_for_a_const_only_parameter_is_e0206`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_invalid_parameter_diagnostics.rs) pin code, message and span for each invalid class). Given positionally or as `title = "…"`. Must be top-level (`E0214`), at most once (`E0213`); missing indicator is `W0001`. The remaining v6 parameters (shorttitle, overlay, …) are verified in the rows below. |
| `indicator(shorttitle=)` | partial | [`indicator_display_params_value_assert_accept_and_reject`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Accepted + const-folded (const string). Acceptance verified (a valid const string compiles clean). **Display-only** — carries no runtime effect in the headless VM (known limit). |
| `indicator(overlay=)` | verified | [`indicator_overlay_value_is_recorded`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`indicator_overlay_wrong_type_is_rejected`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Accepted + type-checked (const bool; wrong type is `E0205`). The folded value is carried to `ProgramMetadata.overlay` for host presentation; it has no effect on computed values. Verified: `overlay=true`/`false` record the value, a string is `E0205`. |
| `indicator(format=)` | partial | [`indicator_display_params_value_assert_accept_and_reject`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Accepted (const string, e.g. `format.percent`). Verified: valid string accepted, a non-string is `E0205`. Display-only (known limit). |
| `indicator(precision=)` | partial | [`indicator_display_params_value_assert_accept_and_reject`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Accepted + const-folded (const int `0..=16`). Verified: in-range accepted, `17` is `E0219`. Display-only (known limit). |
| `indicator(scale=)` | partial | [`indicator_display_params_value_assert_accept_and_reject`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`indicator_scale_tokens_follow_the_overlay_contract`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Accepts the registered const-string `scale.left`/`scale.right`/`scale.none` tokens; a numeric or raw-string substitute is rejected. `scale.none` requires `overlay=true`. Display-only (known limit). |
| `indicator(max_bars_back=)` | partial | [`indicator_max_bars_back_named_arg_is_recorded`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`indicator_max_bars_back_raises_the_ceiling`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`indicator_max_bars_back_is_capped_at_5000`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | The `max_bars_back=N` metadata argument. `N` is a const int in `1..=5000` (`E0219` otherwise), raises the effective history ceiling that run-time offsets are checked against, and contributes to the bounded user-slot ring size used by both execution tiers. A literal offset deeper than `N` raises the ceiling further, so the declaration never rejects one. The compiled backend sizes each slot from its observable reads and carry state; dynamic reads retain the effective ceiling. The row remains partial because exact automatic buffer inference across the language is not established (see docs/runtime.md). |
| `indicator(timeframe=)` | partial | [`indicator_display_params_value_assert_accept_and_reject`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Accepted (const string). Verified: valid string accepted, a bool is `E0205`. No HTF resampling applied to the whole indicator (known limit; use `request.security` for HTF). |

Verified: valid bool accepted, a non-bool is `E0205`. Display/metadata-only (known limit). Accepted only with a `timeframe` argument (`E0204`). |
| `indicator(explicit_plot_zorder=)` | partial | [`indicator_display_params_value_assert_accept_and_reject`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Accepted (const bool). Verified: valid bool accepted, a non-bool is `E0205`. Display-only (known limit). |
| `indicator(max_lines_count=)` | verified | [`indicator_max_lines_count_overrides_the_gc_cap`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`indicator_max_lines_count_out_of_range_is_e0219`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | **honored** — a const int `1..=500` (`E0219` otherwise) that overrides the VM's default per-kind active-`line` GC cap (oldest-first eviction). Verified: `max_lines_count=100` + 120 lines keeps the newest 100. |
| `indicator(max_labels_count=)` | verified | [`indicator_display_params_compile_and_max_counts_const_fold`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | **honored** — overrides the `label` GC cap (const int `1..=500`). Verified: `max_labels_count=20` const-folds into the stored `IndicatorInfo` (same mechanism as `max_lines_count`; see the verified `line` cap test). |
| `indicator(max_boxes_count=)` | verified | [`indicator_display_params_compile_and_max_counts_const_fold`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | **honored** — overrides the `box` GC cap (const int `1..=500`). Verified: `max_boxes_count=30` const-folds into the stored `IndicatorInfo`. |
| `indicator(max_polylines_count=)` | verified | [`indicator_display_params_compile_and_max_counts_const_fold`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | **honored** — overrides the `polyline` GC cap (const int `1..=500`). Verified: `max_polylines_count=40` const-folds into the stored `IndicatorInfo`. |
| `indicator(calc_bars_count=)` | partial | [`indicator_display_params_value_assert_accept_and_reject`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Accepted + const-folded (const int). Verified: valid int accepted, a negative value is `E0219`. No history truncation applied (known limit; this VM keeps full history). |
| `indicator(dynamic_requests=)` | partial | [`indicator_display_params_value_assert_accept_and_reject`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`requests_stay_global_when_a_declaration_turns_dynamic_requests_off`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_rejected_forms.rs) | Accepted (const bool). Verified: valid bool accepted, a non-bool is `E0205`. With `false`, a `request.*()` call in a local scope, including one inside a function called there, is `E0242`, as with `strategy()` and `library()`. |
| `indicator(behind_chart=)` | partial | [`indicator_display_params_value_assert_accept_and_reject`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`indicator_behind_chart_param_compiles`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs) | Accepted (const bool). Verified: valid bool compiles+runs clean, a non-bool is `E0205`. Draws the indicator behind the chart candles — display-only, not modeled by the headless VM (known limit). |
| `strategy()` declaration + `initial_capital`/`default_qty_type`/`default_qty_value`/`commission_type`/`commission_value`/`slippage`/`pyramiding`/`process_orders_on_close` (honored) + `calc_on_every_tick`/`calc_on_order_fills`/`currency`/`margin_long`/`margin_short` (metadata) | partial | [`strategy_declaration_defaults`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`strategy_full_param_surface_const_folds`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`strategy_and_indicator_are_mutually_exclusive`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`golden_strategy_declaration`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`strategy_basic.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/strategy_basic.pine) | The documented parameter names are accepted and const-folded into `StrategyInfo`; only the parameters identified as honored in this row drive broker behavior, while the others remain metadata, the honored params drive the broker emulator (order fills, pyramiding, commission/slippage — see section 2.19 and runtime.md). `strategy()`/`indicator()` mutual exclusion is `E0240`. |
| `library()` declaration | partial | [`a_library_script_compiles_standalone`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`default_alias_is_the_library_name`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`library_import.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/library_import.pine), [`a_library_must_export_something_and_binds_its_arguments_by_name`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_rejected_forms.rs) | A `library(title, overlay?, dynamic_requests?)` script kind; arguments bind by position or by name. Title is a const string (`E0205`); the optional `overlay` bool is type-checked but carries no runtime effect (known limit); `dynamic_requests = false` keeps requests global (`E0242`). Mutually exclusive with `indicator()`/`strategy()` (`E0240`); duplicate is `E0213`. Only in a library may a definition carry `export`. A library must export at least one function, method, type or enum (`E0243`); PineWASM has no exported `const` variables. |
| `//@description`, `//@function`, `//@param`, `//@returns`, `//@type`, `//@field` annotations | partial | [`doc_comment_annotations_compile`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Recognized (parsed + skipped like any `//`-comment; the `//@version`/`//@strategy_alert_message` directives are the only special ones). A script using these doc annotations compiles cleanly — they are accepted, not errors. Metadata is not stored (known limit). |
| `//@strategy_alert_message` | verified | [`strategy_alert_message_annotation_is_parsed_into_metadata`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs) | Parsed as a compiler-annotation directive (like `//@version`) and stored as `StrategyInfo.alert_message` metadata (the last one wins); the default alert message is display-only (known limit). Absent annotation leaves it empty. |

### 1.2 Types

| Feature | Status | Tests | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `float` | verified | [`core_example_produces_na_2_minus1`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`mixed_division_is_float`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`arithmetic_precedence.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/arithmetic_precedence.pine) | Float-form literals (`1.5`) and the OHLCV builtins. Mixed int/float arithmetic promotes the int side (explicit `IntToFloat` coercion, [`mixed_arithmetic_types_as_float_with_visible_coercion`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). |
| `int` | verified | [`int_division_keeps_its_fraction`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`int_overflow_is_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`int_literal_types_as_int`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`int_arithmetic.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/int_arithmetic.pine) | Int-form literals (`42`) are `int` (must fit i64, else `E0110`). `int OP int` is int for `+ - * %`; `int / int` is statically int and keeps its fraction (see [`int_division_is_statically_int_for_every_qualifier`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), and an integer-only consumer reads its whole part ([`an_integer_only_consumer_reads_the_whole_part_of_a_fractional_int`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests/fraction_whole_part.rs)); overflow and division/remainder by zero are `na` (**assumed**, checked arithmetic). Explicit `int` annotations are supported (see 1.4), and `input.int()` is covered in §2.20. |
| `bool` | verified | [`comparisons_and_logic.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/comparisons_and_logic.pine), [`logic_follows_kleene_semantics`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`bool_literals_operators_and_equality_evaluate`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs) | `true`/`false` literals, comparison results, `and`/`or`/`not`, `==`/`!=`, `if` conditions — all value-asserted end-to-end. Explicit `bool` annotations are supported (see 1.4), and `input.bool()` is covered in §2.20. Arithmetic on bool is rejected (`E0207`, [`arithmetic_on_bool_is_rejected`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). *On-chart display of a bool is not rendered — a headless-VM boundary, not a missing language feature.* |
| `string` | verified | [`parse_string_escapes`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`string_concatenation_produces_joined_string`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`string_equality_inequality_and_concat_evaluate`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`golden_strings`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`strings.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/strings.pine), [`multiline_triple_quoted_strings_keep_lines`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`unterminated_triple_quoted_string_is_e0102`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs) | Literals, `==`/`!=` equality, `+` concatenation (series-capable, na-propagating), escape handling, the `str.*` namespace (2.6), and explicit `string` annotations (see 1.4) — value-asserted end-to-end. Escapes limited to `\n` `\t` `\"` `\\`. No lexicographic ordering. Unterminated string is `E0102`. *On-chart display of a string is not rendered — a headless-VM boundary, not a missing language feature.* Multiline `"""..."""`/`'''...'''` strings keep their line breaks and indentation, as the language specifies. |
| `color` | verified | [`color_literal_is_typed_color_const`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`color_literal_equals_itself_and_differs`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`color_named_namespace_and_alpha_evaluate_end_to_end`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`color_program_csil_snapshot`](../crates/pinewasm_csil/src/tests.rs), [`golden_colors`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`colors.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/colors.pine) | `#RRGGBB`/`#RRGGBBAA` literals (the optional alpha byte becomes Pine transparency `t` in `0..=100`), the 17 `color.*` named constants (2.11), `==`/`!=` equality (transparency is part of the value), and use as ternary/`select` branch values, all value-asserted end-to-end (lexer → VM). Runtime value is `Value::Color { r, g, b, t }`; the wire format is a `#RRGGBB[AA]` hex string (see [browser.md](browser.md)). A malformed color literal is `E0229`. The `color.*` constructor/accessor functions (`color.new`/`rgb`/`from_gradient`, `color.r/g/b/t`) are verified end-to-end (see 2.11). *Plotting colors / on-chart color rendering is not modeled — a headless-VM boundary, not a missing language feature.* |
| `array<T>` | verified | [`array_from_infers_element_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`array_of_int_is_not_array_of_float`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`aliasing_two_handles_share_mutations`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`var_array_persists_and_grows_across_bars`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`arrays_core.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/arrays_core.pine) | The `array<T>` type is implemented end-to-end: typed constructors + the array-core operations (2.7) via the namespace call form, with **reference semantics** — assignment copies the handle (aliasing observable), `var` arrays persist and grow across bars, an `na` array method is a structured runtime error. `describe()` renders `array<float>`; `array<int>` ≠ `array<float>` and no implicit element coercion (int→float widening only). Runtime is `Value::Ref(HandleId)` into the per-run `Heap`; `CALL_COLLECTION` opcode ([ir.md](ir.md)), heap/limits/errors/rollback ([runtime.md](runtime.md)). Method syntax (`id.push(x)`) and `<type>` generics (`array.new<float>()`) are now available. An `array<MyType>` may hold **user-defined-type elements** (see `array.new<type>()`), with reference semantics preserved through the array (a mutation of a retrieved element is visible through the array). Explicit `array<T>` annotations are accepted. Directly nested collections are rejected; collections can be nested through a UDT field. Enum element types are accepted: `array.new<MyEnum>()` ([`array_new_accepts_enum_element_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), value-tested by [`enum_collections_store_and_return_enum_members`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs); see the `array.new<type>()` row in 2.7). |
| `matrix<T>` | partial | [`matrix_new_infers_matrix_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`matrix_handle_aliasing_observes_mutation`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`matrix_var_persists_across_bars`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`matrix_new_udt_and_sort_field_overloads`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`matrix.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/matrix.pine) | The `matrix<T>` type is implemented end-to-end via the namespace call form (2.8), with reference semantics (handle-copy aliasing, `var` persistence, `na`-method runtime error). Runtime is `Value::Ref(HandleId)` into the per-run `Heap` (`HeapObj::Matrix { elem_ty, rows, cols, items }`, row-major); scalar and UDT cells are supported for the exercised constructor/sort forms, and `chart.point` cells for construction with an initial value and a `get` read ([`dotted_map_and_matrix_point_reads_select_point_overloads`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_chart_point_coordinates.rs)); the remaining built-in collection types have no dedicated matrix tests, so no claim is made for them, so no claim is made for them. Method syntax (`id.get(r, c)`) and `<type>` generics (`matrix.new<int>(r, c)`) are available. Explicit `matrix<T>` annotations are implemented, so far with compile-time tests only: a variable declaration ([`matrix_and_map_annotations_resolve_to_collection_types`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), a function parameter ([`matrix_and_map_types_in_fields_and_parameters`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), and the rejection of an initializer with another element type ([`mismatched_matrix_annotation_is_an_error`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)) and of a non-matrix argument to a `matrix<float>` parameter ([`declared_user_parameter_types_reject_wrong_argument_types`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_call_binding.rs)); other annotation forms have no test, so no claim is made for them. Enum cells are accepted (`matrix.new<MyEnum>()`, [`matrix_new_accepts_enum_element_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), value-tested by [`enum_collections_store_and_return_enum_members`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs)), and `for...in` iterates a matrix by rows ([`for_in_over_matrix_visits_rows_in_order`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs); see the `for...in` row in 1.6). **Remaining gap, which keeps this row `partial`:** for user-defined cells the value tests from source cover construction, `get`/`set`, `rows`/`columns`, and field sorting, so no claim is made for the other operations on a `matrix<MyType>`. |
| `map<K, V>` | verified | [`map_new_infers_map_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`map_handle_aliasing_observes_mutation`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`map_var_persists_across_bars`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`matrix_map.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/matrix_map.pine) | The `map<K, V>` type is implemented end-to-end via the namespace call form (2.9), with **reference semantics** (handle-copy aliasing, `var` persistence, `na`-method runtime error) and **insertion-ordered** keys/values. Runtime is `Value::Ref(HandleId)` into the per-run `Heap` (`HeapObj::Map { key_ty, val_ty, entries }`); keys are scalar value types, and values are scalar value types or the object types covered in 2.9. `describe()` renders `map<string, float>`. Method syntax (`id.get(k)`) and `<K, V>` generics (`map.new<string, float>()`) are now available. A `map<K, MyType>` may hold **user-defined-type values** (see `array.new<type>()` / [`golden_collections_of_udts`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`map_string_to_udt_constructs`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). Explicit `map<K, V>` annotations are implemented, so far with compile-time tests only: a `var` declaration ([`matrix_and_map_annotations_resolve_to_collection_types`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), a user-defined-type field ([`matrix_and_map_types_in_fields_and_parameters`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), a function parameter ([`generic_matrix_and_map_function_parameters_survive_v5_conversion`](../crates/pinewasm_migrate/tests/golden.rs)), an unknown value type rejected with `E0236` ([`unknown_map_value_type_is_e0236`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), and a user-defined or collection key type rejected with `E0234` ([`non_scalar_map_key_annotation_is_e0234`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)); a `map<MyEnum, V>` variable or field annotation is accepted ([`map_enum_key_annotation_is_accepted`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)); other annotation forms have no test, so no claim is made for them. Enum keys and values are accepted: `map.new<MyEnum, V>()` ([`map_new_accepts_enum_key_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)) and `map.new<K, MyEnum>()` ([`map_new_accepts_enum_value_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), value-tested by [`enum_collections_store_and_return_enum_members`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs). `for [key, value] in m` iterates the entries in insertion order ([`for_in_over_map_visits_entries_in_insertion_order`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs); see the `for...in` row in 1.6). |
| User-defined types (`type` keyword, fields, `.new()`) | partial | [`parse_type_decl_snapshot`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`type_new_and_field_read_type_as_field_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`udt_aliasing_two_vars_one_object`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`udt_copy_is_independent`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`udt_field_write_across_bars_via_var`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`udt_nested_type_build_and_read_two_levels`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`udt_self_referential_linked_list_walk`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`udt_na_object_field_read_is_structured_error`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`udt.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/udt.pine), [`copy_is_a_method_of_user_defined_types`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_shadowing_copy_and_fill.rs), [`an_unset_bool_field_is_false`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_shadowing_copy_and_fill.rs), [`a_field_default_is_a_literal_a_built_in_or_an_enum_member`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_rejected_forms.rs) | `type Name` + an indented typed field list (`float x`, `int y = 5`, `string s`, `bool b`, `color c`, `array<T>` fields, and fields typed as another UDT/enum — including self-referential `Node next`). Instances are **reference values** on the per-run `Heap` (`HeapObj::Object { type_id, fields, table_identity }`, `Value::Ref`): assignment copies the handle (aliasing observable), `var`-held objects persist across bars and roll back on realtime ticks, an unassigned object variable is `na`. Constructed with `Name.new(...)` (positional **and** named args; an omitted field takes its declared default or `na`, and `false` for a `bool` field) and `Name.copy(obj)` (shallow copy = a NEW handle whose fields are copied). Field read `obj.field` and write `obj.field := value` (chained `a.b.field` supported). New CSIL opcodes `NEW_OBJECT`/`COPY_OBJECT`/`GET_FIELD`/`SET_FIELD` ([ir.md](ir.md)); heap/rollback ([runtime.md](runtime.md)). Duplicate type name, duplicate field, and unknown field type are `E0236`; construction/write field-type mismatch and unknown-field/`na`-object access are `E0237`. **Methods and collection support:** UDTs carry `method`s (see the `method` row) and may be the element type of an `array<MyType>` / value type of a `map<K, MyType>` (see `array.new<type>()`), with reference semantics preserved through the collection. UDT-typed annotations, including `TypeName value = na`, are implemented. **Remaining gaps:** instance `obj.copy()` notation has known `E0237` debt and is not requalified here (namespace `Name.copy(obj)` is covered), `input`-of-UDT, complete `matrix<MyType>` operation coverage (construction and field sorting are covered; see the `matrix<T>` row), and per-field history (`obj.field[1]` is `E0209`). `obj.copy()` is the method form of `Name.copy(obj)`. A field default is a literal (a sign before a number included), `na`, a built-in variable or constant, or an enum member; a function call or any other expression is `E0236`. |
| Enum types (`enum` keyword) | verified | [`parse_enum_decl_snapshot`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`enum_member_types_as_enum_const`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`enum_member_equality_drives_ternary`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`enum_switch_selects_arm`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`enum_var_persists_across_bars`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`distinct_enums_do_not_compare`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`enums.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/enums.pine), [`input_enum_defaults_to_member_and_host_override_selects_another`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/inputs.rs), [`an_enum_may_not_take_a_built_in_namespace_name`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_rejected_forms.rs) | `enum Name` + an indented member list (`buy = "Buy"`, optional titles). Members are referenced `Name.member`, are a distinct **nominal** value type (`Type::Enum(name)`; `describe()` renders the enum name), and support `==`/`!=` and `switch` between values of the same enum only (cross-enum or enum-vs-int is `E0207`). A member lowers to its zero-based declaration index as a const int carrying the enum type, so comparison/switch reduce to integer ops in the VM — no new opcode or heap object. `var`-held enums persist across bars and roll back on realtime ticks like any scalar. Duplicate enum name, duplicate member, and unknown member are `E0239`. Enum-typed annotations, including `Signal s = na`, and `input.enum(...)` are implemented; see §1.4 and §2.20. Enum collections are implemented: `array.new<MyEnum>()` ([`array_new_accepts_enum_element_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), `matrix.new<MyEnum>()` ([`matrix_new_accepts_enum_element_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), `map.new<MyEnum, V>()` ([`map_new_accepts_enum_key_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), `map.new<K, MyEnum>()` ([`map_new_accepts_enum_value_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), and a `map<MyEnum, V>` variable or field annotation ([`map_enum_key_annotation_is_accepted`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), value-tested by [`enum_collections_store_and_return_enum_members`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs). An enum element is its member index on the `int` lane at run time while its static type stays the enum, so an element of another type is `E0234` ([`enum_collection_element_of_another_type_is_e0234`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). Other ways to hold an enum value in a collection have no test, so no claim is made for them. An enum may not take the name of a built-in type or namespace (`enum ta` is `E0239`). |

A wrong-type initializer/reassignment is a clear `E0226`/`E0218`. The `line` OBJECTS are still created/mutated via `line.new()`/`line.set_*()`/`line.delete()` (§2.12). |

### 1.3 Type qualifiers

| Feature | Status | Tests | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `const` qualifier | verified | [`qualifier_of_constant_expression_is_const`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`const_qualifier_flows_from_literal_to_binding`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Inferred for literal expressions and asserted in the HIR dump (`const int`); consumed by `indicator`/`plot` title checks (`E0206`). The test pins both the const-literal and the series-derived ends of the lattice. *Note: not every corner of v6's full promotion table is modeled (function-boundary rules) — see the promotion row.* Explicit variable declarations reject stronger initializers and rebinding; const references remain mutable through their contents. Regressions: [`declared_qualifiers_preserve_values_and_input_metadata`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/declared_qualifiers.rs), [`explicit_qualifiers_reject_stronger_initializers_and_const_rebinding`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/declared_qualifiers.rs), [`const_reference_permits_content_changes_but_not_rebinding`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/declared_qualifiers.rs). |
| `simple` qualifier | verified | [`timeframe_values_are_simple_until_history_is_read`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`chart_type_predicates_are_simple_bools`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Produced by chart-context reads (`timeframe.period` → `simple string`); asserted in the HIR dump (the bound variable carries `simple`). Sits between `input` and `series` in the join lattice. Explicit variable declarations accept input/const initializers and reject series initializers or writes; regression: [`declared_qualifiers_preserve_values_and_input_metadata`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/declared_qualifiers.rs). `ticker.*` results take the strongest argument qualifier, so `simple` arguments give a `simple string` and `series` arguments select the `series` overload ([`ticker_results_keep_the_qualifier_of_their_arguments`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_series_accessors.rs)). The `footprint.*` and `volume_row.*` accessors are `series` ([`footprint_accessors_do_not_satisfy_simple_parameters`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_series_accessors.rs)). |
| `input` qualifier | verified | [`input_int_is_input_qualified`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`input_source_is_series_qualified`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`input_string_rejected_as_plot_title_by_lattice`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Produced by the `input.*` interface. Verified: the HIR read carries the `input int` qualifier token; the join keeps `input` stronger than `const` (rejected as a `plot`/`indicator` title, `E0206`) and weaker than `series`. `input.source` reads a series, so it is `series float`, not `input`. |
| `series` qualifier | verified | [`builtins_resolve_as_series`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`const_qualifier_flows_from_literal_to_binding`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | OHLCV/`bar_index` and anything derived from them are series; asserted in the HIR dump (`series float`/`series int`) both for the builtins directly and for a binding derived from `close`. A UDT field read is series too (`p.x` → `GetField .0 : series float`), so it drives per-bar plot and fill colors and cannot satisfy a `simple` parameter: [`udt_field_reads_are_series_qualified`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`plot_colors_read_from_udt_fields_are_series`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_udt_field_qualifier.rs), [`udt_fields_do_not_satisfy_simple_parameters`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_udt_field_qualifier.rs), [`udt_field_simple_length.pine`](../tests/compile-fail/udt_field_simple_length.pine). History results are series regardless of the eligible target qualifier. The seven `chart.is_*` predicates are Simple context variables with direct history and Series history results, including offset zero; `chart.bg_color` and `chart.fg_color` remain Input colors without direct history. Direct literal, built-in values without direct-history capability and enum-member targets remain `E0209`; computed Const/Input/Simple targets materialize per bar ([compound history qualifier regressions](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/compound_history_qualifiers.rs)). Explicit declarations preserve series strength even for input or literal initializers; regression: [`explicit_qualifiers_reject_stronger_initializers_and_const_rebinding`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/declared_qualifiers.rs). |
| Qualifier promotion / join rules (const → input → simple → series) | partial | [`qualifier_join_takes_the_upward_maximum`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`input_qualifier_is_produced_by_input_builtins`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | The modeled join is `max` over the lattice `const < input < simple < series`; the test asserts each pairwise join and two concrete binary expressions (`const + series = series`, `const + input = input`) in the HIR dump. Conditional writes also preserve input/simple control dependencies for outer variables: [`input_controlled_reassignments_cannot_reach_const_only_arguments`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs) covers if/else, nested controls, switch statements/expressions, later writes, and helper/tuple declaration boundaries. Persistent scalar write prefixes and carried-read floors are bounded by the linked tests ([`exact_persistent_admissions_and_named_qualifiers`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/persistent_write_qualifiers.rs)). *The full v6 promotion table across every function-boundary/parameter-context rule is not exhaustively modeled; unknown/helper/loop cases and Boolean recurrence diagnostics remain open, and existing series-controlled write policies are unchanged.* Local consistency safeguards additionally prevent proved helper-controlled or explicitly annotated carried reads from appearing fixed at earlier consumers ([`proved_helper_and_annotated_carried_reads_cannot_freeze_plot_offsets`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/persistent_fixedness.rs)). The helper proof stays within direct calls to unique, local, non-exported zero-argument helpers with a single supported expression; it does not interpret arithmetic or captures and is not a call-graph evaluator. Structured-generic and nesting boundaries have native AST regressions ([`structured_generic_guard_nodes_do_not_borrow_plain_helper_facts`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`helper_body_proofs_share_the_existing_nesting_bound`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). These are local safeguards. |

### 1.4 Declarations & mutability

| Feature | Status | Tests | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `=` declaration | verified | [`core_example_produces_na_2_minus1`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`history_user_var.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/history_user_var.pine) | Declares a new variable; redeclaring in the same scope is `E0211` ([`duplicate_declaration_is_rejected`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`duplicate_declaration.pine`](../tests/compile-fail/duplicate_declaration.pine)). A declaration may shadow a built-in variable with warning `W0006`, unless the script also uses the built-in (`E0211`) ([`shadowing_a_builtin_warns_unless_the_script_also_uses_it`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). |
| `:=` reassignment | verified | [`var_counter_increments_across_bars`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`reassign_from_branch_mutates_the_outer_variable`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`reassign_resolves_to_the_existing_symbol`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`var_counter.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/var_counter.pine), [`a_function_may_not_reassign_its_parameters`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_rejected_forms.rs) | Resolves an existing binding within its permitted function frame (innermost-first); a branch can update an outer binding in that frame. A function or method cannot rebind global or caller bindings, while referenced array/object content mutation remains distinct. Input/simple-controlled writes to an outer variable retain that control qualifier and cannot satisfy const-only parameters ([`input_controlled_reassignments_cannot_reach_const_only_arguments`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs)); series-controlled writes retain series strength. Inferred persistent value scalars distinguish active independent stable writes before every read (minimum simple) from source-identified carried flows (series), including earlier consumer checks: [`exact_persistent_admissions_and_named_qualifiers`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/persistent_write_qualifiers.rs), [`stable_write_prefixes_resolve_actual_engine_offsets_and_input_choices`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/persistent_write_qualifiers.rs). Unsupported closed guards abstain from new backward qualification and retain series-on-write behavior ([`unsupported_closed_guards_abstain_from_new_backwards_qualification`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/persistent_write_qualifiers.rs)); unknown/helper/loop generalization and Boolean recurrence diagnostics remain unimplemented. Undeclared/builtin-series/function targets are `E0217` ([`reassign_undeclared_is_rejected`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`reassign_undeclared.pine`](../tests/compile-fail/reassign_undeclared.pine)); the value type must match, except int → float widening ([`reassign_type_mismatch_is_rejected`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`reassign_int_into_float_var_is_coerced`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`reassign_type_mismatch.pine`](../tests/compile-fail/reassign_type_mismatch.pine)) — `E0218` otherwise. A completed summary also promotes a proved potentially carried main-program `var`/`varip` scalar read to series before consumers even when it has an explicit simple annotation. Initialization/write ceilings and their diagnostics remain in force ([`declaration_ceiling_and_recursive_call_errors_keep_their_owners`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/persistent_fixedness.rs)). Covered readonly values, inferred stable prefixes and proved-dead writes retain fixed values at their sites ([`readonly_stable_prefix_dead_and_ordinary_values_remain_fixed_at_their_sites`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/persistent_fixedness.rs)); helper truth does not discard a dynamic scalar-alias dependency ([`helper_truth_cannot_weaken_a_scalar_alias_dynamic_dependency`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/persistent_fixedness.rs)). Input registration/defaults/order and runtime overrides remain intact ([`carried_input_initializers_keep_registration_defaults_order_and_execution`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/persistent_fixedness.rs)), while a carried annotation supplies no static-request source proof ([`carried_annotations_do_not_retain_a_static_request_source_proof`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/persistent_fixedness.rs)). Effectful helper calls still execute their effects ([`effectful_helpers_keep_their_effects_and_unknown_guard_boundary`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/persistent_fixedness.rs)), and a Series-admitting color field keeps its per-bar paint ([`supported_series_color_consumers_keep_per_bar_paint`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/persistent_fixedness.rs)). A function may not reassign its own parameters either, with `:=` or a compound assignment (`E0217`). |
| Variables per scope (at most 1,000) | verified | [`a_scope_declares_at_most_1000_variables`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_rejected_forms.rs) | A scope may declare at most 1,000 variables; the declaration past the limit is `E0253`. The global scope and each local block (`if`, `switch`, loop and function bodies) count separately, and `_` declarations do not count. |
| `var` declaration mode | verified | [`var_counter_increments_across_bars`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`var_persists_without_reassignment`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`conditional_var_initializes_once_on_first_execution`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`history_of_a_persistent_var`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`var_counter.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/var_counter.pine) | Initialize-once + carry-forward of the previous bar's final value. A `var` inside an `if` branch initializes on the first bar the branch runs (**assumed** nuance, pinned by test); cells before the first init read as na. `var x := e` is a parse error. On a realtime bar a `var` slot rolls back to its bar-start value between ticks; `varip` opts out of that rollback. |
| `varip` declaration mode | verified | [`parse_varip_declares_a_persistent_slot`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`parse_varip_annotated_and_reassign_rules`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`varip_counts_ticks_var_counts_bars`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`varip_persists_var_rolls_back_on_rerun_bar`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`realtime_is_deterministic`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs) | Lowers like `var` (persistent slot, run-once init) but its current cell is excluded from realtime-tick rollback, so it persists across ticks. Otherwise identical to `var`. `varip x := e` is a parse error, like `var`. The canonical demo: a `varip` counter counts ticks while a `var` counter counts bars. |
| Explicit scalar, array, drawing, UDT, and enum type annotations | verified | [`type_annotation_exact_match_types_the_variable`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`float_annotation_coerces_int_initializer`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`annotation_type_mismatch_is_e0226`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`annotated_float_declaration_coerces_int_initializer_at_runtime`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`annotations.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/annotations.pine), [`annotation_mismatch.pine`](../tests/compile-fail/annotation_mismatch.pine), [`udt_typed_variable_annotation_parses`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`drawing_typed_declaration_types_the_variable`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Scalar and `color` keywords, `array<T>`/`T[]`, drawing-object names, UDT names, and enum names fix the declared type and combine with `var`. Matching types are accepted; a `float` annotation coerces an `int` initializer (`IntToFloat`); other mismatches are `E0226`. `matrix<T>` and `map<K, V>` annotations are accepted too ([`matrix_and_map_annotations_resolve_to_collection_types`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), but they have compile-time tests only, so they are outside this row's runtime-verified scope and are tracked in their 1.2 rows, which also record the rejected forms; a `map<MyEnum, V>` variable or field annotation is accepted ([`map_enum_key_annotation_is_accepted`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). |
| `na`-typed declaration (`float x = na`) | verified | [`annotation_with_na_initializer_types_the_variable`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`annotation_declared_type_governs_later_reassignment`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`na_typed_declaration_then_reassign_runs`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`var_na_typed_declaration_persists_across_bars`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs) | For nullable types, the annotation supplies the type the bare `na` adopts. Boolean annotations reject `na` with `E0220`. The declared (annotated) type governs later `:=` checks. |
| Tuple destructuring (`[a, b] = f()`) | verified | [`golden_tuple_destructuring`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`destructure_ta_tuple_declares_targets_and_emits_one_call`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`destructure_arity_too_few_targets_is_e0225`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`bare_tuple_expression_outside_legal_position_is_e0225`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`user_tuple_function_two_sites_are_independent`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`conditional_tuple_function_returns_nested_boolean_branches`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`conditional_tuple_if_coerces_components_preserves_history_and_missing_path`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`conditional_tuple_switch_runs_subject_and_selected_effects_once`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`conditional_tuple_checks_component_types_arity_and_scalar_contexts`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`destructured_target_supports_history`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`parse_destructuring_declaration`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`tuples.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/tuples.pine), [`underscore_may_be_declared_repeatedly_and_never_read`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_discard_and_void_helpers.rs) | Compile-time only (no runtime tuple). Declares its targets with `=`; the RHS can be a tuple-returning `ta.*` call, supported `request.security`, user function, or `if`/`switch` value structure. Conditional components unify types independently; bare `na` components infer from corresponding typed sibling branches without widening integers, while wholly untyped components and explicit `na`/bool combinations are rejected. Evidence: [`conditional_tuple_missing_components_infer_from_corresponding_siblings`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`conditional_tuple_missing_inference_does_not_leak_or_invent_a_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`conditional_tuple_explicit_na_does_not_unify_with_v6_bool`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`conditional_tuple_na_components_preserve_integer_type_and_history`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs). Components share the joined qualifier; conditions and selected effects run once. Arity mismatch, a tuple in an illegal position, and a tuple call used as a scalar are all `E0225`. History works on destructured targets; two call sites keep independent state. See [Tuples](language-support.md#tuples). Unused tuple items may be named `_`, any number of times. |
| Unused variables named `_` | verified | [`underscore_may_be_declared_repeatedly_and_never_read`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_discard_and_void_helpers.rs), [`underscore_cannot_be_read_or_reassigned`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_discard_and_void_helpers.rs), [`a_helper_may_end_by_discarding_a_value`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_discard_and_void_helpers.rs), [`parameters_named_underscore_are_unused`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_discard_and_void_helpers.rs) | Any number of `_` variables may be declared, several in one scope, in a tuple (`[_, b, _] = f()`) or as a loop counter; the initializer is still evaluated. A `_` never enters a scope: reading one is `E0201` and reassigning one is `E0217`. A helper called as a statement may end with a `_` declaration, which gives no value (`E0227` where one is needed). Function parameters named `_` follow the same rule. |
| Block scoping & shadowing in `if` branches | verified | [`shadowing_in_branch_scope_is_allowed`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`branch_local_variable_does_not_escape`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Verified: shadowing an outer binding inside a branch compiles clean; a branch-local does not escape its scope (`E0201`). The exact scope rules are assumed. |
| Several statements on one line (`a = 1, b = 2`) | verified | [`comma_separates_statements_on_one_line`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`trailing_comma_ends_the_statement`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`comma_separated_statements_in_blocks_and_inline_bodies`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`comma_separated_statements_analyze_in_order`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`inline_function_body_with_commas_returns_the_last_value`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Declarations, reassignments and expression statements may share a line, separated by `,`; they run in order, left to right. A single-line function or switch-arm body may be such a list; its last statement is the value. |
| `method` and type keywords as names | verified | [`method_is_a_name_outside_method_definitions`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`var_declaration_may_name_a_type_keyword`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`type_keyword_names_are_ordinary_variables`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | `method` is a keyword only in `method name(` (`method = input.string(...)` declares a variable), and `int`/`float`/`bool`/`string` name variables where no type can be meant (`var int = 1`, `string string = "a"`). |
| Function and method parameters shadow built-in values | verified | [`function_parameter_can_shadow_builtin_volume`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`method_parameter_can_shadow_builtin_volume`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`function_parameter_named_volume_uses_argument_not_builtin_series`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`method_parameter_named_volume_uses_argument_not_builtin_series`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs) | References in the body resolve to the argument, and a variable may shadow one too (see the next row). This row covers the builtin-name shape, not every shadowing diagnostic. |
| Variables shadow built-in variables | verified | [`a_variable_may_shadow_a_built_in_with_a_warning`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_shadowing_copy_and_fill.rs), [`the_compiler_still_reads_a_shadowed_built_in_for_its_own_defaults`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_shadowing_copy_and_fill.rs), [`a_built_in_cannot_be_both_used_and_shadowed`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_shadowing_copy_and_fill.rs), [`a_variable_that_shadows_a_built_in_may_be_reassigned`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_shadowing_copy_and_fill.rs) | A declaration such as `float close = open` shadows the built-in for the rest of its scope, with warning `W0006`.
