### 1.5 Operators

| Feature | Status | Tests | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `+` `-` `*` `/` (numeric) | verified | [`core_example_produces_na_2_minus1`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`int_division_keeps_its_fraction`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`int_division_keeps_its_fraction_in_vm_and_native_wasm`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/differential.rs), [`kept_integer_division_matches_interpreter_in_the_standalone_module`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/pine_int_div_carried.rs), [`large_ints_meeting_a_kept_quotient_stay_exact_or_stop_the_module`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/pine_int_div_carried.rs), [`mixed_division_is_float`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`arithmetic_precedence.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/arithmetic_precedence.pine) | Int and float flavors: `int OP int` is statically int for `+ - * / %`; `/` retains a fractional runtime value (`7 / 2` is 3.5, `math.round(9*3/4)` is 7), mixed promotes to float. Division by zero and non-finite/overflowing results are na ([`division_by_zero_is_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`division_and_modulo_by_zero_are_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`int_overflow_is_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs)) — assumed na behavior. |
| `%` (modulo) | verified | [`modulo_int_and_float`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`division_and_modulo_by_zero_are_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`int_arithmetic.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/int_arithmetic.pine) | Same precedence as `*`/`/`. Result takes the sign of the dividend (**assumed** negative-operand semantics); `% 0` is na. `%` on bools is `E0207` ([`modulo_on_bools_is_rejected`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). |
| `==` `!=` `<` `<=` `>` `>=` | verified | [`all_comparison_lattice_vectors_match_vm_and_importless_native`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/float_comparison_tolerance.rs), [`slots_history_mixed_pairs_missing_and_exact_large_ints_keep_their_boundaries`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/float_comparison_tolerance.rs), [`float_input_defaults_and_active_defaults_agree_with_script_comparisons`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/float_comparison_metadata.rs), [`input_defaults_and_active_order_two_ints_without_a_float_round_trip`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/input_comparison_integer_precision.rs) | Main-program int-int comparisons stay exact as i64; mixed numeric pairs promote the int side. Float equality uses IEEE equality or `abs(lhs-rhs) <= 1e-10`; ordering treats that tolerance band as equality. The 48-pair comparison table and separate Eq/Ne constant guards support finite runtime/folding cases, not universal numeric equivalence. Compiler-produced input/active defaults use the same policy; external active-expression consumers remain unqualified. Matching bool/string types support equality; string ordering remains `E0207`. Missing operands produce ordinary false for all six operators. Crossing/sign builtins retain the separate policy described below. |
| `and` / `or` / `not` | verified | [`logic_follows_kleene_semantics`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`or_short_circuits_a_true_lhs_before_a_faulting_rhs`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`and_short_circuits_a_false_lhs_before_a_faulting_rhs`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`lazy_or_skips_a_stateful_ta_call_in_the_rhs`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`na_lhs_still_evaluates_rhs_for_kleene_logic`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`lazy_bool_csil_snapshot`](../crates/pinewasm_csil/src/tests.rs), [`comparisons_and_logic.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/comparisons_and_logic.pine) | `and`/`or` **short-circuit** (`DUP` + `JUMP_IF_EXACT_*` lowering): the rhs is skipped exactly when the lhs alone decides, so guarded faults (`size == 0 or get(size - 1)`) never fire and stateful rhs calls skip bars (the W0008 behavior; the warning side is pinned by [`lazy_series_boolean_or_rhs_warns_w0008_with_actionable_text_and_span`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). **Limitations:** (1) an `na` lhs is legal (v6 bools cannot be na) and still evaluates the rhs for the strong-Kleene combine. (2) `not` binds looser than comparisons/equality here (`not a > b` = `not (a > b)`); v6 makes `not` a tight level-8 unary operator. |
| `?:` ternary conditional | verified | [`ternary_selects_by_condition`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`nested_ternary_is_right_associative_at_runtime`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`ternary_na_condition_selects_the_else_branch`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`ternary_does_not_advance_unselected_ta_state`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`ternary_size_guard_skips_out_of_bounds_get`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`ternary_hir_snapshot`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`ternary.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/ternary.pine) | Lowest precedence, right-associative. Condition must be bool (`E0207`); branch types unify (mixed int/float → float, an `na` branch adopts its sibling — [`ternary_na_branch_adopts_the_other_branch_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)); an `na` condition selects the else branch (**assumed**, consistent with `if`). Lazy, as in v6: only the selected arm runs (literal-only arms keep the `SELECT` opcode), and series-controlled arms warn `W0004` ([`series_ternary_branch_history_sites_warn_w0004`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). |
| `[]` history-referencing operator | verified | [`user_series_history`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`compound_history_matches_variable_history`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`dynamic_history_offset_reads_the_correct_historical_bar`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`bar_index.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/bar_index.pine) | Slot-backed variables and context reads use their own history; supported computed Const/Input/Simple/Series targets materialize through a hidden slot ([`history_of_compound_expression_materializes_a_hidden_slot`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). The offset is numeric: a non-negative integer literal (compile-time fast path) or an `int`/`float` expression resolved at run time, with fractional values truncated toward zero (see §1.9); a nonnumeric offset is `E0210` ([`history_offset_must_be_numeric`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). See §1.9. |
| Unary `-` | verified | [`parse_precedence_snapshot`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`unary_minus_overflow_is_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`int_division_keeps_its_fraction`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs) | Int → int (checked; negating `i64::MIN` is na), float → float. Runtime values asserted. |
| Unary `+` | verified | [`unary_plus_is_identity`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`unary_plus_on_numeric_is_accepted`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`unary_plus_on_string_is_e0207`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Identity on a numeric operand (`+5 == 5`, `+close` reads close); folds away (no HIR node). A non-numeric operand is `E0207`, mirroring unary `-`. |
| `+` string concatenation | verified | [`string_concatenation_produces_joined_string`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`string_concatenation_propagates_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`string_concat_lowers_to_str_concat_opcode`](../crates/pinewasm_csil/src/tests.rs), [`string_concatenation_types_as_string`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | `string + string → string`, series-capable, na-propagating (`STR_CONCAT` opcode). `string + number` is `E0207` ([`string_plus_number_is_rejected`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). |
| Parenthesized grouping | verified | [`high_low_and_grouping_values`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`parse_precedence_snapshot`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs) | Runtime values asserted for `(high + low) / 2`. |

The `ta.cross`, `ta.crossover`, `ta.crossunder` and `math.sign` functions
retain raw numeric behavior at ±5e-11, while explicit script comparisons treat
each as equal to zero. The fixed VM/native
[`cross_and_sign_policy_is_tested_separately_from_comparison_operators`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/cross_comparison_policy.rs)
vectors pin this finite boundary; it is not a global policy inferred for every
builtin.

### 1.6 Control flow

| Feature | Status | Tests | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `if` / `else if` / `else` statement | verified | [`if_else_hir_snapshot`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`reassign_from_branch_mutates_the_outer_variable`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`else_if_chain.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/else_if_chain.pine), [`parse_else_if_chain`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs) | 4-space (or tab = 4) indented blocks; bad indentation is `E0100` ([`bad_indentation.pine`](../tests/compile-fail/bad_indentation.pine)). Condition must be bool (`E0208`); an na condition is treated as false (assumed). Branches may compute or reassign a series consumed by a top-level output call. Plot-family calls inside a branch are rejected with `E0254`. |
| `if` as an expression (value-producing) | verified | [`if_expression_selects_branch_value`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`if_expression_missing_else_is_na`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`nested_if_expression`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`if_expression_multi_statement_branch`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`if_expression_desugars_to_hidden_result_slot`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`if_expression_na_branch_adopts_other_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`if_switch_expressions.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/if_switch_expressions.pine) | `x = if cond ... else ...` where each branch is an indented block whose last statement supplies the value. Branch types unify like `?:` (mixed int/float → float, an `na` branch adopts its sibling); a missing `else` yields `false` for a scalar Boolean result and `na` for other scalar result types; tuple results receive the corresponding missing value per component; mismatches are `E0207`, a non-bool condition `E0208`, an empty/non-expression branch `E0227`. Desugars to a hidden non-persistent result slot (each branch stores its value; the expression reads the slot). **Limitation:** `if`/`switch` expressions are **lazy** (real blocks — only the taken branch runs). `W0004` fires for `ta.*` in series-controlled branches; input/simple-controlled branches are run-stable and do not warn. This differs from the eager `?:`. A trailing declaration or reassignment supplies its stored value; regression: [`assignment_tails_return_the_value_after_the_write`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/block_return_values.rs). |
| `switch` statement | verified | [`switch_default_must_follow_all_labeled_arms`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs), [`lawful_switch_defaults_preserve_input_registration_order`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs), [`switch_statement_runs_arm_effects`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`switch_statement_desugars_to_if_chain`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`parse_switch_statement_with_block_arm_snapshot`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`if_switch_expressions.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/if_switch_expressions.pine) | `switch [subject]` then indented `label => body` arms (or `=> body` for the default); arm bodies are statement blocks executed for effect. Desugars to an if/else-if chain on `==` comparisons (subject evaluated **once** into a hidden temp). At most one `default`, after every labeled arm (`E0225`); no fallthrough. Subjectless arms accumulate their actual condition qualifiers rather than assuming series control: [`labeled_subjectless_writes_follow_the_actual_condition_qualifier`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs), [`earlier_subjectless_conditions_still_control_later_writes_and_calls`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs), [`labeled_subjectless_stateful_calls_warn_only_for_series_control`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs). A sole default is unconditional even when a subject is supplied; its writes retain the actual value/caller qualifiers and stateful calls do not gain `W0004`. Evidence: [`default_only_switch_writes_do_not_depend_on_unused_subject_qualifiers`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs), [`default_only_switch_calls_are_unconditional_unless_the_caller_is_conditional`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs). |
| `switch` expression | verified | [`switch_default_must_follow_all_labeled_arms`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs), [`lawful_switch_defaults_inherit_every_labeled_arm_control_qualifier`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs), [`switch_expression_with_subject_and_default`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`switch_expression_no_match_is_na`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`subjectless_switch_expression`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`switch_subject_evaluated_once`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`switch_all_na_arms_without_expectation_is_e0220`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`switch_label_not_comparable_to_subject_is_e0207`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`parse_switch_expression_snapshot`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs) | Value-producing `switch`: with a subject (labels constant-comparable, compared with `==`) or subjectless (bool case conditions). Arm values unify like the if-expression; an unmatched switch yields `false` for a scalar Boolean result and `na` for other scalar result types; tuple results receive the corresponding missing value per component (**assumed**). The default, if present, must follow every labeled arm (`E0225`); a sole fallback is unconditional. Subjectless result qualifiers retain their condition dependencies: [`labeled_subjectless_results_keep_condition_qualifiers`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs). [`default_only_switch_results_keep_the_value_qualifier`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs) and [`default_only_switches_preserve_subject_effects_and_input_registration_order`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/control_flow_qualifiers.rs) retain the value qualifier, subject effects and input order. Subject evaluated once; arm bodies are inline expressions or blocks whose last statement is the value. Non-comparable labels / mismatched arms are `E0207`; a subjectless non-bool case is `E0208`; all-`na` arms with no expected type are `E0220`. |
| `once` effect block | partial | [`omitted_and_delayed_conditions_execute_once_per_run`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/once.rs), [`contextual_once_names_remain_available_beside_the_statement`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/once.rs), [`open_ticks_restore_activation_but_keep_varip_effects`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/once.rs), [`once_activation_executes_on_the_native_standalone_route`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/once.rs), [`once_block_gate_uses_the_existing_resolved_script_version`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/tests/once.rs) | V6 `once [bool condition]` takes an indented effect block with no result; omitted condition is true. Each instance activates independently and deactivates when its execution commits; open updates restore activation with ordinary rollback. Fresh runs reset it. `once` remains an identifier outside the contextual header; earlier resolved versions reject the block. Tests cover selected helper, nested, qualifier, failure and tick cases. Broader data-provider/helper/strategy recalculation combinations remain unqualified; existing stateful-loop restrictions still apply. |
| `for` loop | partial | [`for_loop_sums_one_to_n_per_bar`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`loop_tailed_function_returns_last_evaluated_value_or_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`loop_tailed_function_accepts_assignment_as_iteration_value`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_loop_positive_step_uses_descending_bounds`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_loop_negative_step_is_runtime_error`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_loop_zero_step_is_runtime_error`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_loop_over_float_bounds`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`int_start_float_end_keeps_int_counter_and_fractional_comparison`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_end_boundary_is_reevaluated_each_iteration`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_loop_end_to_end_triangular_numbers`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`loops.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/loops.pine), [`for_loop_counter_may_declare_int`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs) | `for i = start to end [by step]`; the positive `by` magnitude follows the initial bounds direction, and nonpositive values compile but stop at runtime with a structured error. The `start` and `step` expressions are evaluated once at entry, and the `end` expression is re-evaluated before each iteration. A counted loop ending a value block returns its latest evaluated final expression or assignment, or `na` if none ran. The counter is loop-local and immutable (`E0217`); an integer start with an implicit/integer step keeps an integer counter even when the end bound is float, which remains fractional in termination comparisons. The local runtime error for a nonpositive step is `UserError`. Direct loop-result declarations, `while` results, nested conditional tails, and stateful `ta.*`/compound history in the body remain unsupported. `for int i = ...` is accepted; the annotation is dropped because the counter type follows from the bounds. |
| `for...in` loop | verified | [`for_in_sums_array_elements`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_index_element_form`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_empty_array_runs_zero_iterations`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_supports_break_and_continue`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_nested_loops`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_binds_element_and_desugars_to_array_get`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_over_non_array_is_e0235`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_over_matrix_binds_rows`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_over_map_binds_keys_and_values`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_over_map_without_key_binding_is_e0235`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_over_matrix_visits_rows_in_order`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_over_map_visits_entries_in_insertion_order`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_over_matrix_and_map_and_enum_collections_match_interpreter`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/differential.rs), [`collections_syntax.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/collections_syntax.pine) | `for element in arr` and `for [index, element] in arr` over an `array<T>`. Desugars to a counted loop over `array.size(arr)` (the iterable is bound once to a hidden temp; the end is re-evaluated before each iteration, so a body that resizes the array or matrix is walked to its updated size, as the loop semantics state: [`for_in_over_a_resized_array_or_matrix_walks_the_updated_size`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs)) binding the element (and index) via `array.get` per iteration; `break`/`continue` and nesting work. An empty array iterates zero times because `array.size > 0` guards the counted loop. `for row in m` and `for [i, row] in m` over a matrix bind each row, top to bottom, as a new array (`matrix.row`, bounded by `matrix.rows`). When the body uses the row only as the receiver of builtin `array.*` calls and methods that neither return, store nor view it (not `array.concat`, which returns its receiver, `array.slice`, a view, or `array.from`), no reference to it can outlive its iteration, so the loop refills one hidden row array instead and holds one row's elements against the collection budget, not one per row ([`for_in_over_a_matrix_reuses_one_row_when_the_row_stays_in_its_iteration`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_over_a_matrix_gives_a_kept_row_its_own_array`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_over_a_matrix_fits_one_row_in_the_budget_in_vm_and_wasm`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/differential.rs)). `for [key, value] in m` over a map visits the entries in insertion order and binds each value from the snapshot taken at entry, so a value the body replaces for a later key is not seen by that key's iteration ([`for_in_over_a_map_binds_each_value_from_the_entry_snapshot`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_over_a_map_binds_snapshot_values_in_vm_and_wasm`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/differential.rs)). **Open:** no test yet pins whether a map value binds the snapshot or the current value. The keys are snapshotted with `map.keys`, and the values with `map.values` only when the body may write a map; otherwise each value is read with `map.get` (equal to the snapshot), so the loop holds one copy of the keys against the collection budget ([`for_in_over_a_map_holds_one_snapshot_against_the_budget`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_over_a_map_snapshots_values_only_when_the_body_may_write_a_map`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_over_a_map_holds_one_snapshot_against_the_budget_in_vm_and_wasm`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/differential.rs), [`for_in_map_keys_feed_map_get_in_vm_and_wasm`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/differential.rs)). A loop over `m.keys()` with `m.get(k)` reads current values ([`for_in_over_a_map_keys_array_reads_live_values_with_map_get`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs)). Adding or removing entries in the body stops the script with a runtime error at the next iteration, as the loop semantics describe ([`for_in_over_map_stops_when_the_body_adds_or_removes_entries`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`for_in_over_a_map_the_body_resizes_stops_in_vm_and_wasm`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/differential.rs)); a change in the last iteration is not detected. A map has no single-binding form (`E0235`). Any other iterable is `E0235`. |
| `while` loop | verified | [`while_loop_counts_with_reassignment`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`infinite_while_true_hits_the_instruction_budget`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`loops.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/loops.pine) | `while cond` with a bool condition (`E0208`, [`while_condition_must_be_bool_e0208`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)), re-checked each iteration. `while true` is cut by the per-bar instruction budget as a structured `InstructionLimitExceeded` — never a hang. |
| `break` | verified | [`break_exits_the_loop_early`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`break_targets_the_innermost_loop_only`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`break_outside_loop.pine`](../tests/compile-fail/break_outside_loop.pine) | Exits the innermost loop. Outside any loop is `E0223` ([`break_and_continue_outside_a_loop_are_e0223`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). |
| `continue` | verified | [`continue_skips_to_the_next_iteration`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`break_and_continue_inside_a_loop_are_ok`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Skips to the innermost loop's next step (`for` counter advance / `while` condition). Outside any loop is `E0223`. |

### 1.7 User-defined functions

| Feature | Status | Tests | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Single-line function declaration (`f(x) => x * 2`) | verified | [`parse_single_line_function_definition`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`single_line_function_inlines_at_the_call_site`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`golden_user_defined_functions`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`functions.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/functions.pine) | Inlined per call site (per-call-site instantiation); no HIR/MIR/CSIL function or call node. A body resolves names where the function is defined (its own parameters and locals, then the globals declared above the definition), never in the scope of a caller it is inlined into, on both the Bytecode Interpreter and Standalone WebAssembly; a global declared below the definition is `E0201` in the body even when the call comes after it, or the built-in of that name with `E0211` for shadowing and using it ([`a_global_declared_below_the_definition_is_unknown_in_the_body`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs), [`a_global_declared_below_is_unknown_even_when_the_caller_sees_it`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs), [`each_function_sees_the_globals_above_its_own_definition`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs), [`a_global_declared_below_with_a_built_in_name_is_the_built_in_in_the_body`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs), [`a_body_reads_the_global_not_a_caller_parameter_of_the_same_name`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs), [`a_block_local_in_the_caller_is_invisible_to_the_body_and_the_default`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs), [`every_level_of_inlining_resolves_at_its_own_definition`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs), [`a_name_only_a_caller_declares_is_unknown_in_the_body`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs), [`standalone_bodies_and_defaults_resolve_at_the_definition`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs)). Arguments evaluated once ([`function_arguments_are_evaluated_once`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)); recursion/arity/nesting/plot-in-body are `E0224`/`E0214` ([`direct_recursion_is_rejected`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`wrong_arity_too_few_is_rejected`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`user_function_required_after_default_omitted_is_e0224`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`nested_function_definition_is_rejected`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`plot_inside_a_function_is_rejected`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`recursive_function.pine`](../tests/compile-fail/recursive_function.pine)); an instantiation-size guard trips as a structured `E0224` ([`expansion_guard_reports_a_structured_error_on_a_bomb`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). Several definitions may share a name when their parameter counts or declared types differ (overloads); a call picks the one its arguments bind to, then the best match of declared types against argument types known before analysis (literals, variables, built-in series, casts, pure built-ins, arithmetic), and a choice that would be a guess is `E0224` instead of a silent pick; one overload may call another, while a same-signature redefinition stays `E0224` ([`local_overloads_resolve_by_argument_count_and_type`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`an_overload_may_call_another_overload_of_its_name`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`a_same_signature_redefinition_is_still_an_error`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`type_only_overloads_follow_built_in_series_and_arithmetic_types`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`an_overload_choice_that_would_be_a_guess_is_an_error`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs)). Calls do not depend on definition order: a call may name a function or method defined below it ([limitation 22](language-support.md#semantic-limitations-from-pine-v6), [`calls_to_user_functions_do_not_depend_on_order`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs)). |
| Multi-line function declaration (indented body) | verified | [`parse_multiline_function_definition_with_locals`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`var_inside_a_function_gets_independent_state_per_call_site`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`ta_call_inside_a_function_gets_independent_state_per_call_site`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`golden_user_defined_functions`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`helpers_called_as_statements_may_end_in_a_void_effect`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_discard_and_void_helpers.rs), [`a_void_helper_still_has_no_value`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_discard_and_void_helpers.rs), [`void_tails_through_methods_and_object_calls_run_as_statements`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_discard_and_void_helpers.rs) | The body's last statement is the return value; each call site instantiates fresh state (`var` counters, `ta.*` windows, hidden history/result slots stay independent). A helper called as a statement may end in a void effect call (`strategy.*`, `log.*`, `alert`, `runtime.error`) or in a call to a user function, method or library function, which is then also called as a statement; using its result is `E0212`. |
| `method` declarations | verified | [`parse_method_def_single_line`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`parse_method_def_multi_line`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`method_call_resolves_and_inlines`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`mutating_method_emits_set_field`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`unknown_method_on_object_is_e0237`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`method_on_unknown_receiver_type_is_e0237`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`duplicate_method_for_type_is_e0224`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`golden_udt_methods`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`udt_methods.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/udt_methods.pine), [`method_receiver_may_have_a_qualifier_and_default`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`method_receiver_qualifier_and_default_are_accepted`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`methods_may_be_declared_on_enums`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_parameter_defaults.rs) | `method f(TypeName this, p2, ...) => body` (single- and multi-line). The `method` keyword precedes the name; the first parameter's declared type is the receiver. `obj.f(args)` resolves to the method (keyed by `(receiver type, name)`) and inlines it with the receiver prepended as the first argument — reusing the plain-function per-call-site instantiation machinery, so a method body's `var`/`ta.*` gets fresh per-call-site state, and reference semantics on the receiver object flow through. Method chaining (`obj.a().b()`) works via a chained `MethodCall` AST node whose receiver is bound to a hidden temp. A pure method returns a scalar; a mutating method writes fields via `:=` (`SetField`) and may return `this`. Verified via a `Point.length()`, a mutating `move(dx, dy)`, chaining, two independent objects, a method calling another method, and per-call-site `var` state. An unknown method on an object receiver is `E0237` (with a did-you-mean); a `method` on an unknown receiver type is `E0237`; a duplicate method for one receiver type is `E0224`. Methods may also be declared on collection receivers (`method f(array<float> this)`). The receiver may carry a qualifier (`method m(simple string s)`) and a default, like any other parameter. The receiver may also be an enum type, and `Enum.member.f()` calls a method on a member. |
| Method dispatch by first-argument type (overloads) | verified | [`same_method_name_on_two_types_does_not_collide`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`a_main_script_method_overloaded_by_its_parameters_dispatches`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`methods_overloaded_by_argument_type_dispatch`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`an_overload_is_chosen_by_the_type_of_a_call_argument`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`a_library_method_overloaded_by_its_parameters_links_each_overload`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`a_repeated_main_script_method_signature_is_still_an_error`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs) | Dispatch keys on the receiver's concrete type: the test defines a method `v` on two distinct types and calls both in one expression with no collision (each `(type, name)` is a distinct method, resolved by first-argument type). One receiver type may also overload a method name by its other parameters, by count or by type (`Scene.add(Mesh)` beside `Scene.add(Label3D)`, `Camera.lookAt(float, float, float)` beside `Camera.lookAt(Vec3)`); a call picks the overload as a function call does. When an argument's type only follows from evaluating it (`s.add(Mesh.new())`, `s.add(f())`), that argument is evaluated once, in order, before the choice. Only a second method with the same signature for one receiver type is `E0224`. A method may also be called as a function with the receiver first (`m(x, ...)`, also as a tuple destructure's right side) when no function of that name exists; the receiver's type picks the method as for `x.m(...)`, and a receiver of another type is `E0237` ([`a_method_may_be_called_as_a_function`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`a_method_called_as_a_function_dispatches_on_the_receiver_type`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`a_method_called_as_a_function_rejects_another_receiver_type`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`a_tuple_returning_method_called_as_a_function_destructures`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs)). |
| Collection method-call syntax (`id.method(args)`) | verified | [`method_call_array_equals_namespace_form`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`method_call_map_equals_namespace_form`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`method_call_matrix_equals_namespace_form`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`method_call_lowers_to_identical_csil_as_namespace_form`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`unknown_method_on_collection_is_e0235`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`method_call_on_non_collection_is_not_a_method`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`collections_syntax.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/collections_syntax.pine) | Postfix `value.method(args)` on an array/matrix/map-typed receiver. Desugars to the namespace form with the receiver as the first argument (`a.push(x)` ≡ `array.push(a, x)`), lowering to byte-identical CSIL. The registry resolves `(receiver namespace, method)` to the same builtin. An unknown method on a collection receiver is `E0235` with a did-you-mean; a method-style call on a non-collection value falls through to the normal unknown-function path. |
| Default parameter values | verified | [`parse_function_default_parameter`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`default_parameter_is_used_when_the_argument_is_omitted`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`a_mistyped_default_is_an_error_at_the_definition`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs), [`a_default_of_the_declared_type_is_accepted_whether_or_not_it_is_used`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs), [`functions.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/functions.pine) | A parameter may declare a default value (`p = 1`); a call may omit trailing defaulted arguments. A typed parameter's default must have the declared type (an `int` default widens to `float`, and `int / int` is `int`). A call that omits the argument reports a mismatch as `E0205` at the default, and adds no second error where the call is itself an argument ([`every_call_that_omits_a_mistyped_default_reports_that_default`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs), [`a_mistyped_default_adds_no_second_error_where_the_call_is_an_argument`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs)). A default that no call evaluates is `E0205` too when it is built from literals alone, for functions, methods, library definitions, an overload no call selects and a method's receiver ([`a_default_is_checked_against_every_kind_of_declared_type`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs), [`a_method_default_is_checked_like_a_function_default`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs), [`a_method_receiver_default_is_checked_though_no_call_can_omit_it`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs), [`the_default_of_an_overload_no_call_selects_is_checked`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs), [`a_library_default_is_checked_whether_or_not_the_importer_calls_it`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs), [`a_library_default_is_held_to_a_type_the_library_declares`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs)); that error follows the script's other errors, and a rejected redefinition is not checked ([`an_unevaluated_default_is_reported_after_the_rest_of_the_script`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs), [`a_rejected_redefinition_is_not_checked_for_its_defaults`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs)). Any other default (`na`, a name, a call, an operator on operands it does not take) is checked only by a call that omits the argument ([`a_default_that_names_a_value_is_checked_where_a_call_omits_it`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs), [`a_default_that_is_not_well_typed_is_left_to_the_call_that_evaluates_it`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/typed_user_parameters.rs)). |
| Named arguments for bound call signatures | verified | [`parse_named_argument_in_call`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`parse_keyword_spelling_as_named_argument`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`parse_positional_after_named_is_error`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`named_arguments_in_any_order`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`unknown_parameter_name_is_e0228_with_suggestion`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`named_argument_on_user_function`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`scalar_request_interfaces_bind_the_canonical_named_parameters`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`named_length_matches_positional_output`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/named_args.rs), [`named_matrix_new_matches_positional_output`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/named_args.rs), [`matrix_new_named_arguments_match_positional_hir`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`named_arguments.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/named_arguments.pine) | Registry-bound built-ins, declarations, scalar `request.*` calls, typed `matrix.new<type>` constructors, and user functions accept `name = value` after positional args, including parameter names that spell a keyword (`to =`). Binding is positional then named; positional-after-named is a parse error, duplicate/unknown names are `E0228`, and an omitted required parameter is `E0204`. Other collection calls and the drawing, footprint, and chart-feed-only host interfaces remain deliberately positional and report their dedicated diagnostics. |
| `export` (library functions/types) | partial | [`exported_function_is_inlined_and_plots_42`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`export_in_an_indicator_is_e0243`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`calling_a_private_function_via_alias_is_e0243`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`calling_an_unknown_export_is_e0243_not_e0202`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`library_overloads_resolve_through_the_alias`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`exported_library_methods_link_in_both_call_forms`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`a_private_library_method_is_not_callable_from_an_importer`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`a_same_signature_redefinition_in_a_library_is_an_error`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`a_repeated_library_method_signature_is_an_error`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`a_library_method_overloaded_by_its_parameters_links_each_overload`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`an_alias_method_call_dispatches_on_the_receiver_type`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`type_only_library_overloads_follow_the_argument_type`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs) | `export` marks a top-level definition importable. Legal only in a `library()` script (`E0243` otherwise); an unknown/private name through an alias is `E0243`, never `E0202`. **Subset:** exported *functions* are importable (including typed/qualified parameters like `series float src`, `simple int n`, `array<color> shades` — the qualifier is dropped, the type recorded), with every overload of a name linked and an alias call choosing among the exported ones. Exported methods link too: an importer calls one on a value of its receiver type (`x.m(...)`) or through the alias with the receiver first (`alias.m(x, ...)`, where the receiver's type picks the method and an unmatched type is `E0243`), its own methods are searched first, and the method body runs in the library's namespace. A second function with the same signature, or a second method with the same signature for one receiver type, is `E0224` at the library's definition, as in a main script. Exported user-defined types and enums link through the import alias, including object constructors, typed object arrays, and methods whose receiver is a library type. Private declarations remain available to library bodies while private names cannot be accessed through an import alias. Separate libraries and aliases retain distinct type identities ([`imported_object_types_keep_identity_and_exported_methods`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`imported_private_object_types_are_not_exposed`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`imported_types_and_enums_are_isolated_across_libraries_and_aliases`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs)). See [`docs/libraries.md`](libraries.md) for the end-to-end import workflow and known limitations. |
| `import` statement | verified | [`exported_function_is_inlined_and_plots_42`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`imported_function_uses_a_series_argument`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`unresolved_import_is_e0243`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`transitive_library_import_links`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`import_cycle_terminates_and_links`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`duplicate_import_alias_is_e0243`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`duplicate_import_path_in_the_libs_vec_is_e0243`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`library_syntax_error_is_attributed_to_the_library_source`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs) | `import Publisher/LibName/Version [as alias]`. Versioned import paths are treated as opaque identifiers and resolve against a caller-provided source map only — never the network (`compile_with_libraries`); an unresolved path is `E0243`, a duplicate alias **or** a duplicate library path is `E0243`. Imports resolve transitively and a cycle terminates. `alias.fn(...)` inlines the exported function per call site exactly like a local one (a `series` arg recomputes per bar). Alias defaults to the library name when `as` is omitted. Each library parses under its own source id (`libraries[i]` → id `i+1`), so a library's parse/analysis diagnostics carry the library's own span. |
| Library independent-compilation (namespace isolation) | verified | [`exported_calls_private_helper_uses_library_helper`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`exported_calls_exported_sibling`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`exported_uses_library_global_not_importer_global`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`importer_same_named_function_still_works_for_the_importer`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`importer_same_named_global_still_works_for_the_importer`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs), [`arguments_evaluate_in_the_caller_namespace`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/libraries.rs) | Each library compiles in an isolated namespace. Synthetic tests prove that exported functions bind the library's own helper functions, exported siblings, and globals even when the importer defines the same names. The importer's own definitions remain unchanged, and call arguments evaluate in the caller's namespace. Parameter defaults resolve in the library's namespace ([`a_library_default_resolves_in_the_library_namespace`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs)), a library function, helper or global initializer sees only the library globals declared above its definition, and one that would read the built-in a later library global shadows is `E0211` ([`a_library_function_sees_only_the_library_globals_above_it`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs), [`library_helpers_and_initializers_follow_their_own_definitions`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs), [`a_library_global_below_with_a_built_in_name_is_rejected_not_replaced`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs)), and a library helper inlined into an exported function reads the library's globals, not that function's parameters ([`a_library_helper_reads_its_library_global_not_an_exported_parameter`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/function_lexical_scope.rs)). |

| Feature | Status | Tests | Notes |

| `x[n]` on identifiers, non-negative integer literal `n` | verified | [`user_series_history`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`history_of_user_variable_works`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`history_user_var.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/history_user_var.pine), [`bar_index.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/bar_index.pine) | Works on builtins and user variables. Reads before bar 0 yield `false` for Boolean history and `na` otherwise. |
| `x[n]` with numeric expression offset | verified | [`dynamic_history_offset_reads_the_correct_historical_bar`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`dynamic_history_offset_that_varies_per_bar`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`dynamic_history_offset_beyond_history_is_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`dynamic_history_offset_negative_is_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`dynamic_history_offset_na_offset_is_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`dynamic_history_offset_on_user_variable`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`dynamic_history_offset_on_compound_expression`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`dynamic_builtin_history_offset_beyond_max_bars_back_errors`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`dynamic_user_slot_history_offset_beyond_max_bars_back_errors`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`history_offset_may_be_an_int_expression`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`golden_dynamic_history_offset`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`dynamic_history_offset.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/dynamic_history_offset.pine) | A numeric `int`- or `float`-typed offset expression (`close[n]`, `close[bar_index-1]`) is resolved at run time: identifier targets lower to `READ_BUILTIN_DYNAMIC`/`READ_USER_SLOT_DYNAMIC` (pop the numeric offset), compound targets materialize into a hidden slot and read it dynamically. `current_bar - offset`; a negative offset (future bar), an offset beyond available history, or an `na` offset yields `false` for Boolean history and `na` otherwise (**assumed** for the negative/`na` cases). A non-negative offset greater than the effective `max_bars_back` ceiling raises `HistoryBufferLimitExceeded`, matching Pine's bounded-buffer error contract for dynamic references. A nonnumeric offset (`string`, etc.) is still `E0210`; fractional offsets truncate toward zero; tests: [`float_typed_history_offsets_truncate_toward_zero`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`history_offset_must_be_numeric`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`history_string_offset.pine`](../tests/compile-fail/history_string_offset.pine). The lowering uses the current CSIL v2 instruction set; it does not require another opcode-version bump. |
| `(expr)[n]` history on compound expressions | verified | [`compound_history_matches_variable_history`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`compound_history_is_na_on_bar_zero`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`history_of_history_composes_like_a_deeper_offset`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`nested_compound_history_hand_computed`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`history_of_compound_expression_materializes_a_hidden_slot`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`compound_history.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/compound_history.pine) | Implicit slot materialization: each site stores into a hidden series slot (`%h0`, ...) and reads it at the offset, so history composes (`close[1][2]` equals `close[3]`). Offsets may be a literal or a numeric expression (the compound-with-dynamic-offset case lowers through `MaterializeHistoryDynamic`; see the row above); direct literal, built-in values without direct-history capability and enum-member targets remain `E0209`; a computed weak-qualifier target is valid. The syntactic site captures its value before later assignments, including when a computed target folds to a variable read ([compound history qualifier regressions](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/compound_history_qualifiers.rs)); `E0215` retired. Sites inside `if` branches warn `W0004` — unavailable history across skipped bars yields `false` for Boolean results and `na` otherwise (**assumed**, pinned by [`compound_history_in_a_branch_reads_gaps_as_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs)). Sites inside `var` initializers execute only once and warn `W0004`; the hidden slot records that evaluation, and the variable retains the initial history result unless reassigned. The result depends on its offset and value type ([`compound_history_in_var_initializer_warns_w0004`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). |
| `max_bars_back()` / history-buffer sizing errors | verified | [`offset_within_default_ceiling_leaves_it_alone`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`offset_beyond_default_ceiling_raises_it`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`literal_offset_and_declared_limit_combine_to_the_larger`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`offset_beyond_hard_limit_is_e0233`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`compound_history_offset_is_also_bounded`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`literal_history_offset_beyond_the_default_buffer_needs_no_declaration`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`literal_history_offset_raises_the_ceiling_for_dynamic_offsets`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`history_offset_beyond_max_bars_back_blocks_compilation_e0233`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`history_offset_exceeds_max_bars_back.pine`](../tests/compile-fail/history_offset_exceeds_max_bars_back.pine), [`dynamic_builtin_history_offset_beyond_max_bars_back_errors`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`dynamic_user_slot_history_offset_beyond_max_bars_back_errors`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs) | V6's buffer-size **error contract** is enforced both statically and dynamically. A literal history offset sizes the buffer itself, as in v6: up to the hard limit of 5000 bars it needs no declaration and raises the effective ceiling (default 500, also raised by `indicator(max_bars_back=)` / `max_bars_back()`; the largest request wins) to its own depth. A literal offset past 5000 is rejected with `E0233`. A dynamic non-negative offset greater than the effective ceiling raises `HistoryBufferLimitExceeded` at runtime. A dynamic offset within the ceiling but before bar 0 yields `false` for Boolean history and `na` otherwise. See §3 and docs/runtime.md. |

## 2. Standard library (v6 namespaces)

This section lists the standard-library namespaces that PineWASM implements.
The Tests column is "—" only for an `unsupported` row (rule 4). The coverage summary
counts no `unsupported` rows, so every row in this section links its evidence.

A handle copied to another variable (`q = p`) names the same plot, also when `p` is a `var` or `varip` that is never reassigned. A `:=` of such a `var`/`varip` anywhere in the script, even below the `fill`, carries into the next bar, so a `fill` that reads it directly or through an alias is `E0205`. A non-handle endpoint is `E0205`, and so is a handle the compiler cannot trace to one call, such as a conditional value or a reassigned variable. The top/bottom gradient overload emits independent top/bottom numeric and color lanes; omitted gradient arguments retain explicit `na` roles. The linked chart path preserves these typed lanes and matches the expected output for constant, dynamic, and missing boundaries with fractional transparency ([`linked_gradient_fills_preserve_numeric_boundaries_and_colors`](../crates/pinewasm_aot/tests/chart_wasm.rs)). On-chart rendering remains a host responsibility, so the overall function remains partial. |

| `timestamp()` | partial | [`timestamp_utc_epoch_ms_is_hand_computed`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`timestamp_timezone_overload_shifts_wall_clock`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`timestamp_na_field_is_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`timestamp_is_na_when_any_calendar_intermediate_overflows`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`timestamp_both_overloads_analyze_and_type_as_int`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`timestamp_wrong_field_type_is_e0205`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`documented_const_input_arguments_accept_expressions_and_aliases`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/inputs.rs), [`iso_date_only_strings_fold_to_utc_midnight`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/timestamp_iso_forms.rs), [`iso_utc_offsets_shift_the_wall_clock`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/timestamp_iso_forms.rs), [`unconfirmed_and_malformed_forms_stay_rejected`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/timestamp_iso_forms.rs), [`timestamp_binds_each_overloads_names`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_named_binding_completion.rs), [`timestamp_timezone_form_takes_optional_clock_fields`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_named_binding_completion.rs), [`timestamp_names_fix_the_overload_and_its_argument_types`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_named_binding_completion.rs) | All three supported forms. `timestamp(year, month, day, hour = 0, minute = 0, second = 0)` and `timestamp(timezone, year, month, day, hour = 0, minute = 0, second = 0)` compute UNIX **ms** via civil-date math (Howard Hinnant `days_from_civil`, exact inverse of the calendar decomposition). `timestamp(dateString)` parses documented English calendar strings at compile time and returns a `const int`, including the `input.time(timestamp("1 Jan 2025 00:00 +0000"), ...)` use case. It also folds the ISO 8601 shape `YYYY-MM-DD`, optionally followed by `Thh:mm[:ss]` and then a `±hh:mm` offset (`"2020-02-20"`, `"2023-11-15T00:00:00-05:00"`); these expectations are pinned by the linked tests. The `Z` designator, a weekday prefix, and a space in place of `T` are rejected (`E0204`). Fixed-offset zones (`"UTC-5"`) and IANA names (`"America/New_York"`, release 2026e, with every daylight-saving change) resolve; the fields are a wall-clock reading, a repeated one read as the earlier instant and a skipped one with the offset before the change ([`a_wall_clock_timestamp_resolves_gaps_and_overlaps`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests/exchange_timezones.rs)); an unknown name stops the run ([`a_timezone_pinewasm_does_not_know_stops_the_run`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests/exchange_timezones.rs)). Out-of-range numeric month/day fields roll over (assumed); an na field or i64 overflow at any step of the calendar arithmetic is na. Arguments may be passed by their parameter names; the names select the overload (`dateString`, `timezone`, or the field form), and a named call may skip the clock fields. |

### 2.3 Time & date

### 2.4 `ta.*` — technical analysis

A large single-return `ta.*` batch is implemented. Two lowering strategies
coexist (see `docs/ir.md`): `ta.change`/`ta.sma` are **slot-based** (one
hidden series slot per call site), and everything else goes through the
**VM-native ta engine** — each call site owns a `TaStateDecl` in the
program's `ta_states` table and lowers to one `CALL_TA` instruction; the VM
keeps ring-buffer/scalar state per site and advances it only when the site
executes (`docs/runtime.md`). Either way the **registry pattern holds**: a
`ta.*` function is a data-driven table entry plus a `TaFunc` data-table row
and a VM state arm — adding the next function does not touch the resolver,
checker, or VM core. Engine functions share the warm-up/na rules documented
in `docs/language-support.md`; const arguments (lengths, flags, alma's
offset/sigma) must be literals (`E0219`,
[`ta_engine_const_argument_violations_are_e0219`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs));
conditional call sites warn `W0004` and freeze their state on skipped bars
([`ta_engine_call_inside_a_branch_warns_w0004`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs),
[`ta_engine_state_freezes_when_its_branch_does_not_execute`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs)).

|---|---|---|---|

IDENT)*` folds into one identifier; number fractions still lex as one token (`1.5`). Only meaningful as built-in references (unresolved dotted names get `E0201`/`E0202` plus a namespacing note; no field access); `ta.sma = 1` is a targeted parse error. |

### 2.5 `math.*`

The pure-builtin framework is implemented: every `math.*` function below is a
data-driven registry entry (typed parameters, variadics, optional defaults,
per-function result rule) behind the single `CALL_PURE` opcode and one VM
dispatch arm — adding the next pure function touches no checker or VM core.
All functions propagate `na` (any `na` argument → `na` result); domain edges
(√−1, log 0, asin 2, int-range overflow) are `na`, never runtime errors
([`math_sqrt_pow_exp_log_hand_computed`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs),
[`math_trig_hand_computed`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs)). The
**windowed** `math.sum` is stateful and implemented by the ta engine
(`CALL_TA`), completing the namespace.

### 2.6 `str.*`

The `str.*` namespace lowers to the `CALL_PURE` opcode (`PureFunc` tags), and
all functions are na-propagating. The two formerly blocked functions are now
implemented: `str.split`
returns an `array<string>` (arrays are implemented — 2.7 — so it rides the
`CALL_COLLECTION` machinery like an array constructor), and `str.format_time`
formats an epoch-ms int against the now-current time axis + timezone handling
(fixed offsets and IANA names) (see `timestamp()` and the `time`/`year`/… builtins in 2.3).

| `str.endswith()` | verified | [`str_contains_startswith_endswith`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`str_predicates_type_as_bool`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | `str.endswith(source, str) → bool`. na-propagating. |

Series format strings are accepted; na format → na; up to `STR_FORMAT_MAX_ARGS` args. |

The documented tokens are written: `yy`/`yyyy`, `M`, `d`, `H`, `h` (12-hour), `m`, `s`, `a`, `S` (milliseconds) and `Z`; other SimpleDateFormat letters such as `E` or `MMM` pass through. A named `timezone` may skip `format`, which then takes its documented default. |

Code-point slice, `begin` inclusive / `end` exclusive; 2-arg form runs to the end. An `end` past the length clamps to it, and `begin` equal to the length yields `""`. A `begin` past the length or an `end` before `begin` is a runtime error. An `na` `begin` counts as 0; an `na` source or explicit `na` `end` yields `na`. |

Grammar: optional sign, digits, single optional `.`; **exponents rejected** (verified `"1e3"` → na). Non-matching → na. |
| `str.tostring()` | partial | [`str_tostring_int_vs_float_formatting_is_exact`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`str_tostring_with_format_string`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`str_tostring_of_na_is_the_string_nan`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`str_tostring_of_an_enum_is_the_member_title`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_discard_and_void_helpers.rs), [`arrays_are_bracketed_and_comma_separated`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_collection_strings.rs), [`matrices_write_one_bracketed_row_per_line`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_collection_strings.rs), [`a_format_applies_to_every_numeric_element`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_collection_strings.rs), [`only_documented_element_types_are_accepted`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_collection_strings.rs), [`collection_strings_reserve_exactly_what_they_write`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`a_const_alias_of_an_enum_member_folds_to_its_title`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_discard_and_void_helpers.rs), [`a_large_enum_writes_its_titles_without_deep_nesting`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_discard_and_void_helpers.rs) | `str.tostring(value[, format]) → string`. Int/float/bool/string values are supported; `na` becomes the string `"NaN"`. Default numeric formatting trims trailing zeros. **Format subset:** `format.mintick`/`format.percent`/`format.volume`/`format.inherit` and selected `DecimalFormat`-style patterns (`#`/`0`, decimal/grouping separators, `%`, literal prefix/suffix), verified by [`unit_format_number_constants`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs) and [`unit_format_pattern_matches_public_number_contract`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs). Series formats are accepted and evaluated each bar. Scalar formatted overloads require int/float values. Full numeric formatting remains incomplete. Arrays and matrices of int, float, bool or string are written as `[a, b, c]`, a matrix as one bracketed row per line, with each element written as its scalar would be and `na` elements as "NaN"; a format applies to every element of an int or float collection. The bracket and row layout is a PineWASM format choice, pinned by tests. The result is charged against the string budget before it is written, or at its written size when a format is given; like the other string-growing operations, a result longer than 1,000,000 bytes is `na`, and the writer stops at that cap. Reading a collection costs one instruction unit per element, plus the format's length per element when a format is given ([`formatting_a_collection_is_charged_per_element_and_pattern_length`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_collection_strings.rs)). An enum argument returns the member's title (its `= "title"`, else its name); a const member, directly or through a const variable, folds to a `const string`, and an `na` enum gives "NaN". |
| `str.trim()` | verified | [`str_trim_strips_ascii_whitespace`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`str_producers_type_as_string`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`str_trim_of_na_is_an_empty_string`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_parameter_defaults.rs) | `str.trim(source) → string`. Strips leading/trailing spaces, tabs, and newlines; interior whitespace preserved. An `na` source gives an empty string. |
| `str.upper()` | partial | [`str_upper_lower_are_ascii_only`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`str_producers_type_as_string`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | `str.upper(source) → string`. ASCII letters only; non-ASCII unchanged. na → na. |

### 2.7 `array.*`

The **array core** is implemented end-to-end: the typed constructors and
the read/write/structural/search operations, via the namespace call form
(`array.push(id, x)`). Method syntax (`id.push(x)`) and `<type>` generics
(`array.new<float>()`) are also supported. Semantics are stated in the notes below; reference semantics (handle aliasing, cross-bar persistence,
na-receiver errors) are documented in [runtime.md](runtime.md) *Collections* and
the opcode in [ir.md](ir.md) (`CALL_COLLECTION`). The implementation adds the numeric
aggregates (`sum`/`avg`/`min`/`max`/`median`/`range`/`mode`/`variance`/`stdev`),
the transforms (`abs`/`standardize`), and ordering (`sort`/`sort_indices` with
the `order.ascending`/`order.descending` constants). The implementation also includes the numeric
surface: `covariance`, the percentile / rank family (`percentrank`,
`percentile_nearest_rank`, `percentile_linear_interpolation`), and the three
binary-search variants. The boolean reductions `array.every`/`array.some` are
also implemented: they take **no**
callback here — they reduce the receiver's own bool/numeric elements to a bool
(`every` = all true, `some` = at least one true; a numeric element is "true"
when non-zero and non-`na`), so they lower to plain linear `CALL_COLLECTION`
reductions like `array.sum`/`array.includes`. The reference-typed constructors
(`array.new_line`/`label`/`box`/`table`/`linefill`) are now verified thin aliases
of the generic `array.new<line>()` etc., each producing a fresh empty
`array<drawing>`. The generic form also accepts a user-defined element type
(`array.new<MyType>()`, value-tested by
[`golden_collections_of_udts`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs)).
An enum element type is accepted
([`array_new_accepts_enum_element_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)).

<summary>Functions</summary>

|---|---|---|---|

### 2.8 `matrix.*`

Matrices are implemented end-to-end via the namespace call form, backed by the same
per-run `Heap` and `CALL_COLLECTION` opcode as arrays and maps
(`HeapObj::Matrix { elem_ty, rows, cols, items }`, row-major). PineWASM accepts
generic constructor syntax such as `matrix.new<int>(...)` for its five
supported scalar element types and also exposes `matrix.new_<T>` names.
Both surface forms lower to the same internal element-type tag. The generic
form also accepts object element types: a user-defined type
(`matrix.new<MyType>(...)`, with construction and field sorting value-tested by
[`matrix_new_udt_and_sort_field_overloads`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs))
and `chart.point`
([`dotted_map_and_matrix_point_reads_select_point_overloads`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_chart_point_coordinates.rs)).
Enum element types are accepted: `matrix.new<MyEnum>()`
([`matrix_new_accepts_enum_element_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)). The remaining built-in
reference types have no matrix test, so no claim is made for them. Tested handle
semantics include assignment aliasing (observable in
[`matrix_handle_aliasing_observes_mutation`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs)),
`var` matrices persist across bars ([`matrix_var_persists_across_bars`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs)),
and calling a method on an `na` matrix is a structured runtime error
([`matrix_na_receiver_is_structured_error`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs)).
Checked index violations, dimension mismatches, and singular inverses use
structured runtime errors. The range-clamping exception in `matrix.fill` is
documented below. Linear-algebra kernels include LU decomposition with partial
pivoting; `inv(A)·A ≈ I` is verified within `1e-3`
([`matrix_inv_times_original_is_identity`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs)).
See [Fixture `matrix.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/matrix.pine).

`matrix.eigenvalues` / `matrix.eigenvectors` are implemented for **symmetric**
matrices via the cyclic Jacobi rotation algorithm (iteration-capped,
deterministic, pure Rust); non-symmetric / non-square input is a structured
error (general and complex spectra are out of scope). `matrix.pinv` is the
Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse for **full-rank** matrices via the normal-equations
form (exact for full column/row rank, reusing the LU inverse); a rank-deficient
input is a structured error (the SVD-based general pseudo-inverse is out of
scope). Numeric `matrix<int>` results currently expose additional return-type
and storage limitations documented in the affected rows below.

### 2.9 `map.*`

Maps are implemented end-to-end via the namespace call form, backed by the same
per-run `Heap` and `CALL_COLLECTION` opcode as arrays. The parser reads the
`<K, V>` type arguments of `map.new<K, V>()`
([`parse_generic_constructor_map_two_type_args`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs)).
For scalar key and value types it rewrites the call to the typed constructor
`map.new_<key>_<val>()` (one name per key/value value-type pairing, still
callable directly), so both spellings lower to identical CSIL
([`generic_constructor_lowers_to_identical_csil_as_typed_form`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs)):
`map.new` with two `ConstInt` type tags the VM decodes. v6
**reference semantics** hold: assignment copies the handle (aliasing
observable), `var` maps persist across bars, and calling a method on an `na` map
is a structured runtime error. Maps are **insertion-ordered** — `map.keys()` and
`map.values()` return arrays in insertion order (verified). Keys are scalar
value types (float/int/bool/string/color) or enum types. Values accept those same
scalar or enum types. Object values require the generic form: a user-defined type
(`map.new<K, MyType>()`, value-tested by
[`golden_collections_of_udts`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs))
or `chart.point`
([`dotted_map_and_matrix_point_reads_select_point_overloads`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_chart_point_coordinates.rs)).
An enum key type or an enum value type is accepted and value-tested
(see the `map.new<K, V>()` row below).
The remaining built-in reference types have no map test, so no claim is made
for them. `map.put` returns the previous value (or `na`); `map.get`/
`map.remove` return the value type (or `na`).

| Feature | Status | Tests | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `map.clear()` | verified | [`map_clear_empties`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`matrix_map.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/matrix_map.pine) | Removes all entries; void. Releases the heap-element budget. |
| `map.contains()` | verified | [`map_contains_true_and_false`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`map_contains_returns_bool`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Membership test → bool. Key type must match the map's key type (`E0234`). |
| `map.copy()` | verified | [`map_copy_is_independent`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`map_copy_returns_same_map_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Shallow copy → a new `map<K, V>`; mutating the copy leaves the source untouched. |
| `map.get()` | verified | [`map_put_get_value_asserted`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`map_get_absent_key_is_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`map_get_returns_value_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | Value for `key` → value type, or `na` if absent. |
| `map.keys()` | verified | [`map_keys_preserve_insertion_order`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`map_keys_order_is_insertion_not_sorted`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`map_keys_returns_array_of_key_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs) | A new `array<K>` of the keys in **insertion order** (verified). |
| `map.new<K, V>()` | verified | [`map_put_get_value_asserted`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`generic_constructor_matrix_and_map`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`parse_generic_constructor_map_two_type_args`](../crates/pinewasm_parse/src/tests.rs), [`generic_constructor_lowers_to_identical_csil_as_typed_form`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`map_new_infers_map_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs), [`map_core_csil_snapshot`](../crates/pinewasm_csil/src/tests.rs), [`golden_collections_of_udts`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/pipeline.rs), [`dotted_map_and_matrix_point_reads_select_point_overloads`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_chart_point_coordinates.rs) | The parser reads the `<K, V>` type arguments and, for scalar key and value types, rewrites the call to the typed `map.new_<key>_<val>()` constructor (identical CSIL); an object value type stays on the generic form. A user-defined value type (`map.new<K, MyType>()`) and a `chart.point` value type (`map.new<K, chart.point>()`) are value-tested, each with a `string` key. The remaining built-in reference types have no map test, so no claim is made for them. Lowers to two `ConstInt` type tags. Takes no runtime arguments (`E0204` otherwise). An enum key type ([`map_new_accepts_enum_key_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)) and an enum value type ([`map_new_accepts_enum_value_type`](../crates/pinewasm_semantics/src/tests.rs)) are accepted, and value-tested by [`enum_collections_store_and_return_enum_members`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs). |

### 2.10 `request.*`

Admitted same/higher-timeframe numeric comparisons use the script float
tolerance. The finite
[`supported_same_and_higher_contexts_use_script_float_comparisons`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/requested_float_comparison_policy.rs)
and publication-clock tests cover that supported subset. The scalar-input request source still receives `E0244`
([`scalar_input_requested_comparison_is_rejected_with_e0244`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/requested_float_comparison_policy.rs));
this change does not widen admission. Requested numeric literals remain `f64`,
so large-int comparisons can differ from exact main-program comparisons
([`existing_float_only_requested_integer_precision_boundary_is_not_represented_as_exact`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/requested_float_comparison_policy.rs)).

Arithmetic and numeric casts over input offsets bind per run, retaining fractional division until index consumption. Offsets controlled by chart series remain unsupported. Float/boolean tuples and tuple-returning helpers with supported immutable numeric locals or bounded pure accumulator loops preserve requested-context component order, history, state, and Boolean types. Loop expansion is capped at 256 iterations, 32,768 nodes and depth 96; constant weights and local array cardinality are supported. External components retain their existing host restrictions. Tuple TA calls, other tuple types, unknown bounds, persistent state, effects and history on loop-mutated locals remain E0244. Reassigned aliases and external-symbol input-selected compounds remain E0244. External-symbol expressions lower to canonical host selectors, including supported compound arithmetic/history forms; missing host lanes read `na`. Current-feed provenance survives ordinary aliases and function parameters instead of relying on sentinel string values. An unchanged chart-base-currency alias plus a literal suffix resolves an external host symbol per run; persistent or reassigned aliases remain restricted. `timeframe.period`, empty strings, and unchanged aliases resolve against the runtime chart feed; direct string inputs use each run's host override, including in external-symbol selectors. Const-qualified nonempty string aliases retain literal selectors. All eight v6 parameters bind, but unsupported nondefault `currency` and `calc_bars_count` values are rejected rather than ignored. The engine still supports only the documented expression/timeframe subset, so this row is partial. Calendar `W` grouping and separation from `7D`: [`security_calendar_week_preserves_unit_and_distinct_memo_keys`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`calendar_weeks_align_partial_weeks_without_using_the_first_bar`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/resample.rs), [`multiweek_calendar_restarts_by_monday_year_and_uses_host_date_identity`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/resample.rs). Chart and request closing paths separate OHLCV aggregation from publication and defer missing-day results; without those clocks, partial-feed publication remains a runtime limitation. Clock behavior and host validation: [`security_authoritative_clocks_defer_missing_days_and_hide_incomplete_bars`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`authoritative_clocks_reject_incomplete_or_inconsistent_host_lanes`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`authoritative_clock_storage_is_budgeted_and_realtime_append_fails_closed`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs). ### 2.11 `color.*`

Color `==` and `!=` expressions produce `series bool`, including comparisons
between constant colors. That qualifier propagates through aliases, functions,
ternaries, and color constructors, so a color selected by such a comparison
cannot feed `hline()`'s input-only color argument. The first-party
[`hline_theme_qualifiers`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/hline_theme_qualifiers.rs)
tests cover those rejection boundaries, accepted input colors and numeric
channel comparisons, and unchanged runtime comparison values.

<summary>Functions & constants (all 17 named constants verified; 7 constructor/accessor functions verified)</summary>

### 2.12 `line.*`

<details>
<summary>Functions & constants</summary>

| Feature | Status | Tests | Notes |

| `line.new()` | partial | [`line_new_setters_getters_roundtrip`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`drawing_point_overloads_read_the_point_coordinates`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`drawings.pine`](../tests/compile-pass/drawings.pine) | A heap reference object registered in the active registry (oldest-first GC, default max 50). The coordinate form and `chart.point.from_index` overload are value-tested; named `xloc`/`extend`/`color`/`style`/`width` are accepted. The point overload selects `point.time` or `point.index` from `xloc` and copies scalar coordinates. Point and supplied option operands are captured once inside the call expression; conditional and persistent initialization stay at the call site. Declared point fields, parentheses and supported collection reads select the point overload. See [`v6_chart_point_coordinates.rs`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_chart_point_coordinates.rs) for value and effect tests. Arbitrary user-function-return overload inference remains unreviewed. Serialized in `drawings.lines`. |

| `table.new()` | partial | [`table_new_setters_and_serialized_state`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`same_position_table_new_replaces_cells_and_makes_old_aliases_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests.rs), [`retired_table_cell_reaches_the_alias_lifetime_marker`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/table_displaced_cell.rs), [`retired_table_calls_on_open_ticks_do_not_break_alias_rollback`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests/table_displacement.rs), [`displacement_keeps_identity_separate_from_na_observations`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests/table_displacement.rs) | The current [table signature](https://www.tradingview.com/pine-script-reference/v6/) is implemented through `force_overlay`, and table-level fields are serialized. Same-position replacement releases the earlier cell grid and makes aliases na-observable while retaining runtime-only displacement provenance. Ordinary unavailable-value cases, lifetime tokens and rollback have local tests; the [table identity test](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/retired_table_identity.rs) separately pins two displaced identities. Native AOT declines same-position replacement and uses the hosted VM. Cell-count constraints remain unimplemented; general VM memory budgets apply. |
| `table.cell()` | partial | [`cell_replacement_resets_all_omitted_attributes`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/v6_drawing_default_contracts.rs), [`retired_table_cell_reaches_the_alias_lifetime_marker`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/table_displaced_cell.rs), [`literal_table_na_still_aborts_before_the_marker9_message`](../crates/pinewasm_compiler/tests/table_displaced_cell.rs), [`only_full_table_cell_distinguishes_displacement_from_literal_na`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests/table_displacement.rs), [`displaced_alias_provenance_survives_containers_fields_and_helper_returns`](../crates/pinewasm_vm/src/tests/table_displacement.rs) | Full calls define all attributes, resetting omitted ones to defaults. The displaced-by-replacement receiver completes without changing the replacement table; arguments still evaluate.
